{"parentUuid":"02a4db2a-cced-43ae-b02a-b89ef27fa6f9","isSidechain":false,"promptId":"55c56ec9-3990-4c96-bb16-48d2cb614d9d","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01GNCdiXFuPoHC1SiXy1cNAR","type":"tool_result","content":"{\"body\":\"## Parent\\n\\n#1\\n\\n## What to build\\n\\nAdmins manage Users. The Users area lists every User with their role and whether they're active. An Admin can create a User (name, email, role, initial password), change a User's role, and deactivate or reactivate a User. An Admin can't deactivate or demote themselves, so the club always has an Admin. Users are never deleted, so every Post and Page keeps its Author.\\n\\n## Acceptance criteria\\n\\n- [ ] An Admin sees all Users with name, email, role and active status.\\n- [ ] An Admin can create a User; the new User can sign in with the initial password, which is stored only as an argon2 hash.\\n- [ ] Creating a User with an email already in use (case-insensitive) is a form error.\\n- [ ] An Admin can change another User's role, and the new permissions apply on that User's next request.\\n- [ ] An Admin can deactivate another User, who then can't sign in; reactivating restores access.\\n- [ ] An Admin trying to deactivate or demote themselves is refused with an explanation.\\n- [ ] A Deactivated User's Posts keep their status and their Author.\\n- [ ] There is no way to delete a User.\\n- [ ] An Editor gets 403 on every Users route, including a direct POST.\\n\\n## Blocked by\\n\\n- #2 (T01)\",\"comments\":[],\"state\":\"OPEN\",\"title\":\"T07: User management (Admin only)\"}\n9\tOPEN\tT08: Seed demo content and the dashboard\tready-for-agent, ticket\t2026-09-29T19:44:17Z\n8\tOPEN\tT07: User management (Admin only)\tready-for-agent, ticket\t2026-09-29T19:44:13Z\n7\tCLOSED\tT06: Pages and Navigation (Admin only)\tready-for-agent, ticket\t2026-10-04T16:21:34Z\n6\tCLOSED\tT05: Confirmation screens\tready-for-agent, ticket\t2026-10-04T16:09:33Z\n5\tCLOSED\tT04: Events and the Menu on the public site\tready-for-agent, ticket\t2026-09-30T01:49:44Z\n4\tCLOSED\tT03: Publish a Post and Export it to the live site\tready-for-agent, ticket\t2026-09-30T01:22:18Z\n3\tCLOSED\tT02: Create and edit a Draft Post\tready-for-agent, ticket\t2026-09-29T19:55:03Z\n2\tCLOSED\tT01: Login and the two roles\tready-for-agent, ticket\t2026-09-29T19:49:26Z\n1\tOPEN\tSpec: Golf & Country Club of Trujillo CMS\tready-for-agent, spec\t2026-09-29T19:37:26Z","is_error":false}]},"uuid":"8bf6421f-5e21-4c39-b5c1-a62c9fd7eadc","timestamp":"2026-10-04T16:22:07.010Z","toolUseResult":{"stdout":"{\"body\":\"## Parent\\n\\n#1\\n\\n## What to build\\n\\nAdmins manage Users. {"parentUuid":"95cfe397-e802-4411-9a9a-1b2e9623eca3","isSidechain":false,"promptId":"55c56ec9-3990-4c96-bb16-48d2cb614d9d","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01PTEUuCVNpyxexh1Rda2wPk","type":"tool_result","content":"<persisted-output>\nOutput too large (30.4KB). Full output saved to: /home/gianlucabocanegra/.claude/projects/-home-gianlucabocanegra-iphs400-mp2-cms/a17197df-d097-41a3-848d-9167a7749ca0/tool-results/becy9x18u.txt\n\nPreview (first 2KB):\n## Problem Statement\n\nThe Golf & Country Club of Trujillo announces news, events and the weekly restaurant menu through WhatsApp groups and Instagram. As a Deactivated User, I want my login to be refused, so that former staff can't change the site.\n...\n</persisted-output>","is_error":false}]},"uuid":"3ef44aef-d675-4452-9b11-2d5407b1c6b0","timestamp":"2026-10-04T16:22:11.352Z","toolUseResult":{"stdout":"## Problem Statement\n\nThe Golf & Country Club of Trujillo announces news, events and the weekly restaurant menu through WhatsApp groups and Instagram. Messages get buried, they're hard to search, and there's no single place that's always up to date. Members keep asking the office the same questions.\n\nThe office wants to post an update in a few minutes without asking anyone for help. The club has three fears:\n- a staff member publishes an unfinished post, or a wrong menu or price, by mistake;\n- someone who should only post news changes the rules or membership Pages, or adds or removes Users;\n- a Draft shows up on the public site.\n\n## Solution\n\nA small CMS with two halves, following ADR-001 and ADR-002.\n\n- **Admin console** (English, local only). Editors write and publish Posts of three kinds: News, Event and Menu. Admins also manage Pages (rules, membership, about) and Users. Every change Members will see needs an explicit Confirmation, with a preview.\n- **Public site** (Spanish, static). Each Export renders Published content into `site/`, and it is deployed to GitHub Pages. The generated Home page shows the Current menu, Upcoming Events and the latest News. Navigation adds three fixed feeds (News, Events, Menu archive) and the Admin's Pages in the order the Admin sets.\n\nThe developer runs the Export, never the office. The console shows how many changes are not yet Live, so nobody confuses Published with Live.\n\nVocabulary follows `CONTEXT.md` throughout.\n\n## User Stories\n\n### Login and session\n\n1. As a User, I want to sign in with my email and password, so that only club staff can reach the console.\n2. As a User, I want a wrong email or password to give the same generic error, so that nobody can find out which emails have accounts.\n3. As a User, I want to sign out, so that the next person at the office computer isn't working as me.\n4. As a Deactivated User, I want my login to be refused, so that former staff can't change the site.\n5. As an Admin, I want a User I deactivate to lose access on their very next request, even if they're already signed in, so that deactivation takes effect immediately.\n6. As a visitor who isn't signed in, I want every console address to send me to the login screen, so that no console content leaks.\n7. As the club, I want passwords stored only as argon2 hashes and never logged, so that a copied database doesn't reveal them.\n8. As the club, I want every form that changes something to carry a CSRF token, so that another website can't make a signed-in User's browser change content.\n\n### Roles\n\n9. As an Editor, I want the console to show me only Posts, the dashboard and signing out, so that I'm not distracted by things I can't use.\n10. As the club, I want an Editor who types a Page or User address directly to be refused with a \"not allowed\" response, so that the restriction is real and not just a hidden link.\n11. As an Admin, I want to do everything an Editor can, plus manage Pages and Users, so that one person can run the whole site.\n\n### Posts\n\n12. As an Editor, I want to create a Post by choosing its kind (News, Event or Menu), so that it appears in the right place on the public site.\n13. As an Editor, I want to write a Post body in Markdown, so that I can add headings, lists and links without a complicated editor.\n14. As an Editor, I want a new Post to start as a Draft, so that nothing reaches Members until I decide.\n15. As an Editor, I want the Slug generated from the title with accents stripped (\"Menú de la semana\" → `menu-de-la-semana`), so that I never have to think about addresses.\n16. As an Editor, I want to edit the Slug before first publish, so that I can shorten an awkward one.\n17. As a Member, I want a Slug to never change once content has been Published, even after it's Unpublished and republished, so that links shared on WhatsApp keep working.\n18. As an Editor, I want a clash with an existing Slug to be fixed automatically by adding a number (`-2`, `-3`), so that I can reuse a common title such as \"Menú de la semana\" every week.\n19. As an Editor, I want a clear error if I type a Slug that's already taken, so that I don't overwrite someone else's address.\n20. As an Editor, I want to preview a Post as Members will see it (Spanish public layout, sanitized Markdown), so that I catch mistakes before they go Live.\n21. As an Editor, I want to edit, publish, unpublish or delete any Post, including one written by another Editor, so that whoever is in the office can fix Friday's menu on Monday.\n22. As the club, I want the Author to stay the original writer when someone else edits a Post, so that the console records who created it.\n23. As an Admin, I want to do anything to Posts that an Editor can, so that I can cover for the office.\n\n### Events\n\n24. As an Editor, I want to give an Event a start date, an optional start time and an optional end date, so that a dinner at 8 pm and a Saturday–Sunday tournament are both described precisely.\n25. As an Editor, I want an Event without a start date to be rejected, so that every Event can be sorted.\n26. As an Editor, I want an end date earlier than the start date to be rejected with a clear message, so that impossible ranges never go Live.\n27. As a Member, I want the Event date shown first and prominently on every Event, so that I can tell at a glance when it is.\n28. As a Member, I want Upcoming Events listed soonest first, so that \"when is the next event?\" is answered at the top.\n29. As a Member, I want a weekend tournament to stay Upcoming until its end date has passed, so that I can still find it while it's happening.\n\n### Menu\n\n30. As an Editor, I want to post each week's menu as a new Menu Post, so that updating it takes a few minutes.\n31. As a Member, I want the Home page to show only the Current menu, so that I never read last week's prices by mistake.\n32. As a Member, I want older Menus in a Menu archive, so that I can look up what was served before.\n33. As an Editor, I want the previous Menu to leave the Home page automatically when the new one goes Live, so that nobody has to remember to unpublish it.\n\n### Confirmation\n\n34. As an Editor, I want publishing to go through a Confirmation screen showing the preview, so that an unfinished post or a wrong price isn't published by one careless click.\n35. As an Editor, I want saving edits to Published content to go through the same Confirmation, so that a price change to a Live menu is checked before it goes out.\n36. As an Editor, I want unpublishing to need Confirmation, so that I don't accidentally take down something Members rely on.\n37. As an Editor, I want deleting to need Confirmation that says it's permanent, so that I don't lose content by mistake.\n38. As an Editor, I want to cancel on the Confirmation screen and return to my unsaved edits, so that I can fix the mistake I just noticed.\n39. As an Editor, I want saving a Draft to need no Confirmation, so that everyday writing stays quick.\n\n### Pages\n\n40. As an Admin, I want to create, edit, preview, publish, unpublish and delete Pages (rules, membership, about) with the same Draft/Published lifecycle and Confirmation as Posts, so that I only learn one workflow.\n41. As an Admin, I want to give each Page a navigation order number, so that Members see the Pages in a sensible order.\n42. As a Member, I want every Live Page to appear in the Navigation, so that I can find the rules and membership information.\n43. As the club, I want a Page Slug that clashes with a public-site address (`index`, `noticias`, `eventos`, `menus`, `style`) to be rejected, so that a Page can never overwrite the Home page or a feed.\n\n### Users\n\n44. As an Admin, I want to create a User with a name, email, role and initial password, so that new office staff can start posting.\n45. As an Admin, I want to change a User's role between Admin and Editor, so that responsibilities can change.\n46. As an Admin, I want to deactivate and reactivate a User, so that former staff lose access without their content or authorship being lost.\n47. As an Admin, I want to be prevented from deactivating or demoting myself, so that the club always has an Admin.\n48. As an Admin, I want to see all Users with their role and whether they're active, so that I know who has access.\n49. As the club, I want Users never to be deleted, so that every Post and Page keeps its Author.\n\n### Dashboard and content lists\n\n50. As a User, I want a dashboard with counts of Posts by kind and status, and (for Admins) Pages by status, so that I can see the state of the site at a glance.\n51. As a User, I want the dashboard to show how many changes are not yet Live and when the last Export happened, so that I know when to ask for an Export.\n52. As a User, I want to filter the Post list by kind and by status, so that I can find this week's Draft menu quickly.\n53. As a User, I want the Post list to show title, kind, status, Author and last-updated time in Lima time, so that I can tell what changed recently.\n54. As a User, I want status filters that match no content to still appear, showing zero, so that the list doesn't change shape the way WordPress's did.\n\n### Public site\n\n55. As a Member, I want a Home page with the Current menu, the next Upcoming Events and the latest News, so that I can find what I need in a few seconds.\n56. As a Member, I want News, Events and Menu archive feeds in the Navigation, so that I can browse each kind of Post.\n57. As a Member, I want the Events feed to show Upcoming Events first and then past Events, most recent first, so that the list stays useful after an event is over.\n58. As a Member, I want the public site entirely in Spanish, dates included (\"29 de septiembre de 2026\"), so that it reads naturally.\n59. As a Member, I want every date and time shown in Lima time, so that a post never appears dated tomorrow.\n60. As a Member, I want News and Menus dated by when they were first published, so that the date means \"when the club said this\".\n61. As the club, I want no Author names on the public site, so that the club speaks as one voice.\n62. As the club, I want Drafts never to appear on the public site in any form (page, feed entry, Home page or Navigation), so that the club's third fear can't happen.\n63. As the club, I want user-written Markdown sanitized before it's rendered, so that a pasted `<script>` can't run in a Member's browser.\n64. As an Editor, I want to embed an image hosted elsewhere (for example on the club's Instagram) through a Markdown image link, so that posts can have pictures without uploads.\n65. As a Member, I want the site to work from its GitHub Pages subfolder, so that every link and stylesheet loads.\n\n### Export\n\n66. As the developer, I want an Export to render only Published content into `site/` and deploy it, so that what Members see is exactly what is Published.\n67. As the developer, I want each Export to decide Upcoming from the export date in Lima time, so that the result is predictable.\n68. As the developer, I want each Export to record when it ran, so that the console can count changes that aren't Live yet.\n69. As a grader, I want the seed script to create an Admin, an Editor and demo content (at least one Draft and one Published item of each kind, including a multi-day Event), so that the CMS can be tried right away from a fresh clone.\n\n## Implementation Decisions\n\n### Modules\n\n- **Accounts:** Users, argon2 hashing, login and logout, the signed session cookie, and the rule that a Deactivated User is refused both at login and on every request.\n- **Permissions:** the role check shared by all console routes. Editor: Posts and the dashboard. Admin: everything. It's enforced server-side, and a refusal returns 403.\n- **CSRF:** a per-session token, a hidden field in every state-changing form, and a check on every non-GET console request.\n- **Content:** Posts and Pages, their Draft/Published lifecycle, Slug generation, clash handling and locking, Event-date validation, and the queries for Current menu, Upcoming and the feeds. The console and the Export share it, so both use the same definitions.\n- **Markdown rendering:** one function, used by both Preview and Export, that turns Markdown into sanitized HTML (markdown-it, then nh3). It allows `img` only with an `http(s)` source.\n- **Console routes and templates:** login, dashboard, Post list and editor, Page list and editor, Users, and Confirmation screens. The console is in English.\n- **Export:** `render_site` reads the database and writes the Spanish public site. The `cms publish` and `cms deploy` commands together make up an Export.\n- **Time:** helpers for UTC storage, America/Lima display (standard-library `zoneinfo`) and Spanish date formatting from a hard-coded month list. No new dependency.\n\n### Schema (SQLite; no ORM, following ADR-002's stack)\n\n- **users:** id, name, email (unique, case-insensitive), password_hash, role (`admin` | `editor`), active, created_at.\n- **posts:** id, kind (`news` | `event` | `menu`, fixed at creation), title, slug, body_md, status (`draft` | `published`), author_id, created_at, updated_at, first_published_at, and for Events only: event_start_date, event_start_time (nullable), event_end_date (nullable). The slug is unique within a kind.\n- **pages:** id, title, slug (unique), body_md, status, nav_order, author_id, created_at, updated_at, first_published_at.\n- **exports:** the time each Export ran, plus a count of Published items deleted since then, used by the not-yet-Live indicator.\n- All timestamps are stored in UTC.\n\n### Rules\n\n- **Slug:** generated from the title, lower-case ASCII with accents stripped and hyphens between words. An auto-generated clash gets a numeric suffix; a slug typed by hand that clashes is a form error. The slug can be edited only while `first_published_at` is empty, and once set, that field is never cleared, even by Unpublishing.\n- **Reserved Page Slugs:** `index`, `noticias`, `eventos`, `menus`, `style`.\n- **Event date:** a start date is required. The end date must be on or after the start date. Event fields are rejected on News and Menu Posts.\n- **Upcoming:** `coalesce(event_end_date, event_start_date) >= export_date`, where the export date is a date in America/Lima. The Home page shows the next 3 Upcoming Events.\n- **Current menu:** the Published Menu with the latest `first_published_at`. Every other Published Menu goes to the Menu archive, newest first.\n- **Home page:** the Current menu, the next 3 Upcoming Events and the latest 5 News, all on one page.\n- **Confirmation (no JavaScript):** submitting a change that Members will see (publish, unpublish, delete, or saving Published content) returns a Confirmation page. That page shows a summary and, where relevant, the rendered preview, and carries the pending values in hidden fields with the CSRF token. Only a second POST that includes the confirm flag applies the change. Cancel returns to the editor with the values intact. Saving a Draft applies immediately.\n- **Self-protection:** a request that would deactivate the Admin making it, or change that Admin's own role, is refused with an explanation.\n- **Not-yet-Live indicator:** Posts and Pages whose `updated_at` is after the last Export, plus Published items deleted since then. If there has never been an Export, every Published item counts.\n- **First Admin:** the seed script creates the first Admin and Editor, taking their passwords from `CMS_ADMIN_PASSWORD` and `CMS_EDITOR_PASSWORD`. No separate bootstrap command.\n\n### Public site layout (all links relative)\n\n- `index.html`: the Home page.\n- `noticias/index.html` and `noticias/<slug>.html`: News.\n- `eventos/index.html` and `eventos/<slug>.html`: Events (Upcoming, then past).\n- `menus/index.html` and `menus/<slug>.html`: the Menu archive and each Menu.\n- `<slug>.html`: Pages.\n- `style.css`.\n- Navigation order: Inicio, Noticias, Eventos, Menús anteriores, then Pages by nav_order (ties broken by title).\n- The site title comes from `CMS_SITE_TITLE`, set to the club's name in `.env.example`.\n\n### Interfaces that change\n\n- `create_app()` accepts a database path (default from settings), so tests can give each app its own database.\n- `render_site(out, export_date=None)` reads Published content from the configured database. `export_date` defaults to today in America/Lima and records the Export.\n\n## Testing Decisions\n\n- **A good test** exercises behaviour through a public seam and asserts only what a User or Member could observe: status codes, redirects, rendered console HTML, and the files and HTML written to `site/`. Tests never query tables or call internal helpers. That lets the storage and module layout change without rewriting tests.\n- **Seam 1, the console over HTTP:** `create_app()` driven by the existing `client` and `client_as(\"admin\" | \"editor\")` fixtures. This covers login and deactivation, Permissions (Editor gets 403 on Pages and Users), CSRF rejection, the Post and Page lifecycle, Slug rules, Event-date validation, Confirmation (the first POST changes nothing; the confirmed POST applies), User management and self-protection, the dashboard counts and filters.\n- **Seam 2, Export:** `render_site(tmp_path, export_date=...)`, asserting on the output files. This covers Drafts never being written, the Current menu versus the Menu archive, the Upcoming boundary (the weekend tournament on its last day, and the day after), Navigation order, Spanish dates in Lima time, no Author names, sanitized Markdown (a `<script>` is stripped and an external image survives), and relative paths only.\n- **Fixture change:** each test gets a fresh temporary database seeded with the `DEMO_USERS` admin and editor from the conftest file, so `client_as` works and tests never share state.\n- **Prior art:** `tests/test_t00.py`, which checks the admin page answers over HTTP, that `render_site` writes files, and that paths are relative. `tests/conftest.py` provides the `client_as` pattern. The T00 tests must keep passing.\n- No tests below these two seams.\n\n## Out of Scope\n\n- Media uploads. Images are only Markdown links to files hosted elsewhere.\n- Review or approval workflows, and any status beyond Draft and Published.\n- Scheduled publishing.\n- The office running Exports; there's no Export button in the console.\n- JavaScript on the public site, including client-side Upcoming filtering.\n- Member logins, comments and search.\n- Self-service password change and password reset.\n- Deleting Users.\n- Changing a Post's kind after creation.\n- Multi-site, drag-and-drop page builders and WYSIWYG editors (excluded by the assignment).\n- A Spanish admin console.\n- Deploying the admin console anywhere public (ADR-001).\n\n## Further Notes\n\n- The CLI keeps the assignment's command names. In the glossary, running `cms publish` and then `cms deploy` is one Export. The word \"publish\" in the console only ever means the Draft → Published status change.\n- A stale Upcoming list between Exports is accepted: the fix is to Export often, not to add JavaScript.\n- Confirmation is the club's main defence against a wrong price. It applies to Published content edits as well as status changes, because a Live Menu is most often corrected by editing it.\n- Every console route must require login. = \"iphs400-mp2-cms\"\nversion = \"0.1.0\"\ndescription = \"IPHS 400 MP2 — a small web CMS with a local admin console and a static public site\"\nrequires-python = \">=3.12\"\ndependencies = [\n    \"fastapi>=0.115\",\n    \"uvicorn[standard]>=0.32\",\n    \"jinja2>=3.1\",\n    \"python-multipart>=0.0.12\",   # form posts\n    \"itsdangerous>=2.2\",          # signed session cookies\n    \"argon2-cffi>=23.1\",          # password hashing (never store plain passwords)\n    \"markdown-it-py>=3.0\",        # Markdown -> HTML\n    \"nh3>=0.2\",                   # HTML sanitizer (strips <script> etc.)\n    \"ghp-import>=2.1\",            # push site/ to the gh-pages branch\n]\n\n[dependency-groups]\ndev = [\"pytest>=8.3\", \"httpx>=0.28\"]\n\n[project.scripts]\ncms = \"app.cli:main\"\n\n[tool.pytest.ini_options]\ntestpaths = [\"tests\"]\n\n[build-system]\nrequires = [\"hatchling\"]\nbuild-backend = \"hatchling.build\"\n\n[tool.hatch.build.targets.wheel]\npackages = [\"app\"]\n6-- a staff member publishes an unfinished post, or a wrong menu or price, by mistake;\n7:- someone who should only post news changes the rules or membership Pages, or adds or removes Users;\n8-- a Draft shows up on the public site.\n--\n13-\n14:- **Admin console** (English, local only). Editors write and publish Posts of three kinds: News, Event and Menu. Admins also manage Pages (rules, membership, about) and Users. Every change Members will see needs an explicit Confirmation, with a preview.\n15-- **Public site** (Spanish, static). Each Export renders Published content into `site/`, and it is deployed to GitHub Pages. The generated Home page shows the Current menu, Upcoming Events and the latest News. Navigation adds three fixed feeds (News, Events, Menu archive) and the Admin's Pages in the order the Admin sets.\n--\n20-\n21:## User Stories\n22-\n--\n24-\n25:1. As a User, I want to sign in with my email and password, so that only club staff can reach the console.\n26:2. As a User, I want a wrong email or password to give the same generic error, so that nobody can find out which emails have accounts.\n27:3. As a User, I want to sign out, so that the next person at the office computer isn't working as me.\n28:4. As a Deactivated User, I want my login to be refused, so that former staff can't change the site.\n29:5. As an Admin, I want a User I deactivate to lose access on their very next request, even if they're already signed in, so that deactivation takes effect immediately.\n30-6. As a visitor who isn't signed in, I want every console address to send me to the login screen, so that no console content leaks.\n31-7. As the club, I want passwords stored only as argon2 hashes and never logged, so that a copied database doesn't reveal them.\n32:8. As the club, I want every form that changes something to carry a CSRF token, so that another website can't make a signed-in User's browser change content.\n33-\n--\n36-9. As an Editor, I want the console to show me only Posts, the dashboard and signing out, so that I'm not distracted by things I can't use.\n37:10. As the club, I want an Editor who types a Page or User address directly to be refused with a \"not allowed\" response, so that the restriction is real and not just a hidden link.\n38:11. As an Admin, I want to do everything an Editor can, plus manage Pages and Users, so that one person can run the whole site.\n39-\n--\n86-\n87:### Users\n88-\n89:44. As an Admin, I want to create a User with a name, email, role and initial password, so that new office staff can start posting.\n90:45. As an Admin, I want to change a User's role between Admin and Editor, so that responsibilities can change.\n91:46. As an Admin, I want to deactivate and reactivate a User, so that former staff lose access without their content or authorship being lost.\n92-47. As an Admin, I want to be prevented from deactivating or demoting myself, so that the club always has an Admin.\n93:48. As an Admin, I want to see all Users with their role and whether they're active, so that I know who has access.\n94:49. As the club, I want Users never to be deleted, so that every Post and Page keeps its Author.\n95-\n--\n97-\n98:50. As a User, I want a dashboard with counts of Posts by kind and status, and (for Admins) Pages by status, so that I can see the state of the site at a glance.\n99:51. As a User, I want the dashboard to show how many changes are not yet Live and when the last Export happened, so that I know when to ask for an Export.\n100:52. As a User, I want to filter the Post list by kind and by status, so that I can find this week's Draft menu quickly.\n101:53. As a User, I want the Post list to show title, kind, status, Author and last-updated time in Lima time, so that I can tell what changed recently.\n102:54. As a User, I want status filters that match no content to still appear, showing zero, so that the list doesn't change shape the way WordPress's did.\n103-\n--\n113-62. As the club, I want Drafts never to appear on the public site in any form (page, feed entry, Home page or Navigation), so that the club's third fear can't happen.\n114:63. As the club, I want user-written Markdown sanitized before it's rendered, so that a pasted `<script>` can't run in a Member's browser.\n115-64. As an Editor, I want to embed an image hosted elsewhere (for example on the club's Instagram) through a Markdown image link, so that posts can have pictures without uploads.\n--\n128-\n129:- **Accounts:** Users, argon2 hashing, login and logout, the signed session cookie, and the rule that a Deactivated User is refused both at login and on every request.\n130-- **Permissions:** the role check shared by all console routes. Editor: Posts and the dashboard. Admin: everything. It's enforced server-side, and a refusal returns 403.\n--\n133-- **Markdown rendering:** one function, used by both Preview and Export, that turns Markdown into sanitized HTML (markdown-it, then nh3). It allows `img` only with an `http(s)` source.\n134:- **Console routes and templates:** login, dashboard, Post list and editor, Page list and editor, Users, and Confirmation screens. The console is in English.\n135-- **Export:** `render_site` reads the database and writes the Spanish public site. The `cms publish` and `cms deploy` commands together make up an Export.\n--\n139-\n140:- **users:** id, name, email (unique, case-insensitive), password_hash, role (`admin` | `editor`), active, created_at.\n141-- **posts:** id, kind (`news` | `event` | `menu`, fixed at creation), title, slug, body_md, status (`draft` | `published`), author_id, created_at, updated_at, first_published_at, and for Events only: event_start_date, event_start_time (nullable), event_end_date (nullable). The slug is unique within a kind.\n--\n176-\n177:- **A good test** exercises behaviour through a public seam and asserts only what a User or Member could observe: status codes, redirects, rendered console HTML, and the files and HTML written to `site/`. Tests never query tables or call internal helpers. That lets the storage and module layout change without rewriting tests.\n178:- **Seam 1, the console over HTTP:** `create_app()` driven by the existing `client` and `client_as(\"admin\" | \"editor\")` fixtures. This covers login and deactivation, Permissions (Editor gets 403 on Pages and Users), CSRF rejection, the Post and Page lifecycle, Slug rules, Event-date validation, Confirmation (the first POST changes nothing; the confirmed POST applies), User management and self-protection, the dashboard counts and filters.\n179-- **Seam 2, Export:** `render_site(tmp_path, export_date=...)`, asserting on the output files. This covers Drafts never being written, the Current menu versus the Menu archive, the Upcoming boundary (the weekend tournament on its last day, and the day after), Navigation order, Spanish dates in Lima time, no Author names, sanitized Markdown (a `<script>` is stripped and an external image survives), and relative paths only.\n180:- **Fixture change:** each test gets a fresh temporary database seeded with the `DEMO_USERS` admin and editor from the conftest file, so `client_as` works and tests never share state.\n181-- **Prior art:** `tests/test_t00.py`, which checks the admin page answers over HTTP, that `render_site` writes files, and that paths are relative. `tests/conftest.py` provides the `client_as` pattern. The T00 tests must keep passing.\n--\n192-- Self-service password change and password reset.\n193:- Deleting Users.\n194-- Changing a Post's kind after = \"iphs400-mp2-cms\"\nversion = \"0.1.0\"\ndescription = \"IPHS 400 MP2 — a small web CMS with a local admin console and a static public site\"\nrequires-python = \">=3.12\"\ndependencies = [\n <script> etc.)\n    \"ghp-import>=2.1\",            # push site/ to the gh-pages branch\n]\n\n[dependency-groups]\ndev = [\"pytest>=8.3\", \"httpx>=0.28\"]\n\n[project.scripts]\ncms = = [\"app\"]\n6-- a staff member publishes an unfinished post, or a wrong menu or price, by mistake;\n7:- someone who should only post news changes the rules or membership Pages, or adds or removes Users;\n8-- a Draft shows up on the public site.\n--\n13-\n14:- **Admin console** (English, local only). Editors write and publish Posts of three kinds: News, Event and Menu. Admins also manage Pages (rules, membership, about) and Users. Every change Members will see needs an explicit Confirmation, with a preview.\n15-- **Public site** (Spanish, static). Each Export renders Published content into `site/`, and it is deployed to GitHub Pages. The generated Home page shows the Current menu, Upcoming Events and the latest News. Navigation adds three fixed feeds (News, Events, Menu archive) and the Admin's Pages in the order the Admin sets.\n--\n20-\n21:## User Stories\n22-\n--\n24-\n25:1. As a User, I want to sign in with my email and password, so that only club staff can reach the console.\n26:2. As a User, I want a wrong email or password to give the same generic error, so that nobody can find out which emails have accounts.\n27:3. As a User, I want to sign out, so that the next person at the office computer isn't working as me.\n28:4. As a Deactivated User, I want my login to be refused, so that former staff can't change the site.\n29:5. As an Admin, I want a User I deactivate to lose access on their very next request, even if they're already signed in, so that deactivation takes effect immediately.\n30-6. As a visitor who isn't signed in, I want every console address to send me to the login screen, so that no console content leaks.\n31-7. As the club, I want passwords stored only as argon2 hashes and never logged, so that a copied database doesn't reveal them.\n32:8. As the club, I want every form that changes something to carry a CSRF token, so that another website can't make a signed-in User's browser change content.\n33-\n--\n36-9. As an Editor, I want the console to show me only Posts, the dashboard and signing out, so that I'm not distracted by things I can't use.\n37:10. As the club, I want an Editor who types a Page or User address directly to be refused with a \"not allowed\" response, so that the restriction is real and not just a hidden link.\n38:11. As an Admin, I want to do everything an Editor can, plus manage Pages and Users, so that one person can run the whole site.\n39-\n--\n86-\n87:### Users\n88-\n89:44. As an Admin, I want to create a User with a name, email, role and initial password, so that new office staff can start posting.\n90:45. As an Admin, I want to change a User's role between Admin and Editor, so that responsibilities can change.\n91:46. As an Admin, I want to deactivate and reactivate a User, so that former staff lose access without their content or authorship being lost.\n92-47. As an Admin, I want to be prevented from deactivating or demoting myself, so that the club always has an Admin.\n93:48. As an Admin, I want to see all Users with their role and whether they're active, so that I know who has access.\n94:49. As the club, I want Users never to be deleted, so that every Post and Page keeps its Author.\n95-\n--\n97-\n98:50. As a User, I want a dashboard with counts of Posts by kind and status, and (for Admins) Pages by status, so that I can see the state of the site at a glance.\n99:51. As a User, I want the dashboard to show how many changes are not yet Live and when the last Export happened, so that I know when to ask for an Export.\n100:52. As a User, I want to filter the Post list by kind and by status, so that I can find this week's Draft menu quickly.\n101:53. As a User, I want the Post list to show title, kind, status, Author and last-updated time in Lima time, so that I can tell what changed recently.\n102:54. As a User, I want status filters that match no content to still appear, showing zero, so that the list doesn't change shape the way WordPress's did.\n103-\n--\n113-62. As the club, I want Drafts never to appear on the public site in any form (page, feed entry, Home page or Navigation), so that the club's third fear can't happen.\n114:63. As the club, I want user-written Markdown sanitized before it's rendered, so that a pasted `<script>` can't run in a Member's browser.\n115-64. As an Editor, I want to embed an image hosted elsewhere (for example on the club's Instagram) through a Markdown image link, so that posts can have pictures without uploads.\n--\n128-\n129:- **Accounts:** Users, argon2 hashing, login and logout, the signed session cookie, and the rule that a Deactivated User is refused both at login and on every request.\n130-- **Permissions:** the role check shared by all console routes. Editor: Posts and the dashboard. Admin: everything. It's enforced server-side, and a refusal returns 403.\n--\n133-- **Markdown rendering:** one function, used by both Preview and Export, that turns Markdown into sanitized HTML (markdown-it, then nh3). It allows `img` only with an `http(s)` source.\n134:- **Console routes and templates:** login, dashboard, Post list and editor, Page list and editor, Users, and Confirmation screens. The console is in English.\n135-- **Export:** `render_site` reads the database and writes the Spanish public site. The `cms publish` and `cms deploy` commands together make up an Export.\n--\n139-\n140:- **users:** id, name, email (unique, case-insensitive), password_hash, role (`admin` | `editor`), active, created_at.\n141-- **posts:** id, kind (`news` | `event` | `menu`, fixed at creation), title, slug, body_md, status (`draft` | `published`), author_id, created_at, updated_at, first_published_at, and for Events only: event_start_date, event_start_time (nullable), event_end_date (nullable). The slug is unique within a kind.\n--\n176-\n177:- **A good test** exercises behaviour through a public seam and asserts only what a User or Member could observe: status codes, redirects, rendered console HTML, and the files and HTML written to `site/`. Tests never query tables or call internal helpers. That lets the storage and module layout change without rewriting tests.\n178:- **Seam 1, the console over HTTP:** `create_app()` driven by the existing `client` and `client_as(\"admin\" | \"editor\")` fixtures. This covers login and deactivation, Permissions (Editor gets 403 on Pages and Users), CSRF rejection, the Post and Page lifecycle, Slug rules, Event-date validation, Confirmation (the first POST changes nothing; the confirmed POST applies), User management and self-protection, the dashboard counts and filters.\n179-- **Seam 2, Export:** `render_site(tmp_path, export_date=...)`, asserting on the output files. This covers Drafts never being written, the Current menu versus the Menu archive, the Upcoming boundary (the weekend tournament on its last day, and the day after), Navigation order, Spanish dates in Lima time, no Author names, sanitized Markdown (a `<script>` is stripped and an external image survives), and relative paths only.\n180:- **Fixture change:** each test gets a fresh temporary database seeded with the `DEMO_USERS` admin and editor from the conftest file, so `client_as` works and tests never share state.\n181-- **Prior art:** `tests/test_t00.py`, which checks the admin page answers over HTTP, that `render_site` writes files, and that paths are relative. `tests/conftest.py` provides the `client_as` pattern. The T00 tests must keep passing.\n--\n192-- Self-service password change and password reset.\n193:- Deleting Users.\n194-- Changing a Post's kind after creation.","stderr":"","interrupted":false,"isImage":false,"noOutputExpected":false},"serverClassifierContext":{"request":"e51d1b69-f6b3-4333-93d4-1e4e91cae975","context":{"git_state":{"cwd":"/home/gianlucabocanegra/iphs400-mp2-cms","root":"/home/gianlucabocanegra/iphs400-mp2-cms","branch":"main","default_branch":"main","status":{"clean":false,"counts":{"staged":0,"modified":1,"untracked":null,"untracked_normal":6},"porcelain":null,"truncated":false},"visibility":{"origin":{"host":"github.com","remote":"gianlucabocanegra/iphs400-mp2-cms","visibility":"public"},"push_remote":"origin","remotes":[{"name":"origin","host":"github.com","remote":"gianlucabocanegra/iphs400-mp2-cms","visibility":"public"}],"visibility_cache":[{"host":"github.com","remote":"gianlucabocanegra/iphs400-mp2-cms","visibility":"public"}]}},"live_cwd":"/home/gianlucabocanegra/iphs400-mp2-cms","platform":"wsl"}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"66256e54-87a4-49ae-bbcd-79f60fcb3146","session_id":"478fac9b-daa9-4ab2-9906-272b5c8a3a43","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/home/gianlucabocanegra/iphs400-mp2-cms","sessionId":"a17197df-d097-41a3-848d-9167a7749ca0","version":"2.1.289","gitBranch":"main"}

{"parentUuid":"d391c13a-b617-4dbc-97f0-9077eb655c58","isSidechain":false,"promptId":"55c56ec9-3990-4c96-bb16-48d2cb614d9d","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_017rGiBLTe56R5XPrdffdFqZ","type":"tool_result","content":"\"\"\"Users: argon2 hashing, authentication, and Deactivation.\n\nUsers are never deleted, only Deactivated (see CONTEXT.md).\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport sqlite3\nfrom datetime import datetime, timezone\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nfrom argon2 import PasswordHasher\nfrom argon2.exceptions import InvalidHashError, VerificationError\n\nfrom app import db\n\n_hasher = PasswordHasher()\n# Verified against when the email is unknown, so a miss costs as much as a hit.\n_DUMMY_HASH = _hasher.hash(\"not-a-real-password\")\n\n\ndef create_user(conn: sqlite3.Connection, *, name: str, email: str,\n                password: str, role: str) -> None:\n    conn.execute(\n        \"INSERT INTO users (name, email, password_hash, role, created_at) \"\n        \"VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?)\",\n        (name, email.strip(), _hasher.hash(password), role,\n         datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat()),\n    )\n    conn.commit()\n\n\ndef authenticate(conn: sqlite3.Connection, email: str,\n                 password: str) -> sqlite3.Row | None:\n    \"\"\"The active User with this email and password, else None (never says why).\"\"\"\n    user = conn.execute(\"SELECT * FROM users WHERE email = ?\",\n                        (email.strip(),)).fetchone()\n    stored = user[\"password_hash\"] if user else _DUMMY_HASH\n    try:\n        _hasher.verify(stored, password)\n    except (VerificationError, InvalidHashError):\n        return None\n    if user is None or not user[\"active\"]:\n        return None\n    return user\n\n\ndef get_user(conn: sqlite3.Connection, user_id: int) -> sqlite3.Row | None:\n    return conn.execute(\"SELECT * FROM users WHERE id = ?\", (user_id,)).fetchone()\n\n\ndef end_sessions(conn: sqlite3.Connection, user_id: int) -> None:\n    \"\"\"Invalidate every session cookie issued to this User.\"\"\"\n    conn.execute(\"UPDATE users SET session_version = session_version + 1 \"\n                 \"WHERE id = ?\", (user_id,))\n    conn.commit()\n\n\ndef set_active(path: Path | str, email: str, active: bool) -> None:\n    \"\"\"Deactivate or reactivate a User. Their content and authorship stay put.\"\"\"\n    conn = db.connect(path)\n    try:\n        conn.execute(\"UPDATE users SET active = ? WHERE email = ?\",\n                     (int(active), email.strip()))\n        conn.commit()\n    finally:\n        conn.close()\n\n\ndef seed_users(path: Path | str, *, admin_password: str, editor_password: str,\n               admin_email: str = \"admin@example.test\",\n               editor_email: str = \"editor@example.test\") -> None:\n    \"\"\"Create the first Admin and Editor. Existing emails are left alone.\"\"\"\n    db.init_db(path)\n    conn = db.connect(path)\n    try:\n        for name, email, password, role in (\n            (\"Demo Admin\", admin_email, admin_password, \"admin\"),\n            (\"Demo Editor\", editor_email, editor_password, \"editor\"),\n        ):\n            exists = conn.execute(\"SELECT 1 FROM users WHERE email = ?\",\n                                  (email,)).fetchone()\n            if not exists:\n                create_user(conn, name=name, email=email, password=password,\n                            role=role)\n    finally:\n        conn.close()\n\"\"\"Permissions and CSRF: the checks shared by every route.\n\n- `csrf_protect` runs on every state-changing request in the app.\n- `current_user` is what makes a console route require login, and refuses a\n  Deactivated User on every request.\n- `require_admin` is the role check for Admin-only areas. Refusal is a 403.\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport hmac\nimport secrets\nimport sqlite3\nfrom typing import Iterator\n\nfrom fastapi import Depends, HTTPException, Request\n\nfrom app import accounts, db\n\nSAFE_METHODS = {\"GET\", \"HEAD\", \"OPTIONS\"}\n\n\nclass LoginRequired(Exception):\n    \"\"\"Raised when a request needs a signed-in User and has none.\"\"\"\n\n\ndef get_db(request: Request) -> Iterator[sqlite3.Connection]:\n    conn = db.connect(request.app.state.db_path)\n    try:\n        yield conn\n    finally:\n        conn.close()\n\n\ndef csrf_token(request: Request) -> str:\n    \"\"\"This session's CSRF token, created on first use.\"\"\"\n    token = request.session.get(\"csrf\")\n    if not token:\n        token = request.session[\"csrf\"] = secrets.token_urlsafe(32)\n    return token\n\n\nasync def csrf_protect(request: Request) -> None:\n    if request.method in SAFE_METHODS:\n        return\n    sent = request.headers.get(\"x-csrf-token\")\n    if sent is None:\n        sent = (await request.form()).get(\"csrf_token\")\n    expected = request.session.get(\"csrf\")\n    if (not expected or not isinstance(sent, str)\n            or not hmac.compare_digest(sent, expected)):\n        raise HTTPException(403, \"Invalid or missing CSRF token.\")\n\n\ndef sign_in(request: Request, user: sqlite3.Row) -> None:\n    # Fresh session and token on login, so nothing set before it carries over.\n    request.session.clear()\n    request.session[\"user_id\"] = user[\"id\"]\n    request.session[\"session_version\"] = user[\"session_version\"]\n    request.session[\"csrf\"] = secrets.token_urlsafe(32)\n\n\ndef current_user(request: Request,\n                 conn: sqlite3.Connection = Depends(get_db)) -> sqlite3.Row:\n    user_id = request.session.get(\"user_id\")\n    user = accounts.get_user(conn, user_id) if user_id else None\n    if (user is None or not user[\"active\"]\n            or user[\"session_version\"] != request.session.get(\"session_version\")):\n        request.session.clear()\n        raise LoginRequired()\n    return user\n\n\ndef require_admin(user: sqlite3.Row = Depends(current_user)) -> sqlite3.Row:\n    if user[\"role\"] != \"admin\":\n        raise HTTPException(403, \"This area is for Admins only.\")\n    return user\n\"\"\"Page list, editor and Preview. Admin only: the router-level check covers every route.\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport sqlite3\n\nfrom fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, Form, HTTPException, Request\nfrom fastapi.responses import RedirectResponse\n\nfrom app import auth\nfrom app import pages as store\nfrom app.markdown import render_markdown\nfrom app.web import templates\n\nrouter = APIRouter(prefix=\"/pages\", dependencies=[Depends(auth.require_admin)])\n\nEDITOR_FIELDS = (\"title\", \"slug\", \"body_md\", \"nav_order\")\nBLANK = {\"title\": \"\", \"slug\": \"\", \"body_md\": \"\", \"nav_order\": \"0\"}\n\n\ndef form_page(request: Request, user: sqlite3.Row, page: dict, *,\n              error: str | None = None, status_code: int = 200):\n    return templates.TemplateResponse(\n        request, \"admin/page_form.html\",\n        {\"title\": \"Edit page\" if page.get(\"id\") else \"New page\", \"user\": user,\n         \"page\": page, \"error\": error},\n        status_code=status_code)\n\n\ndef load(conn: sqlite3.Connection, page_id: int) -> sqlite3.Row:\n    page = store.get_page(conn, page_id)\n    if page is None:\n        raise HTTPException(404, \"Page not found\")\n    return page\n\n\nasync def pending_values(request: Request) -> dict | None:\n    \"\"\"The editor's values as submitted, or None if the form carried none.\"\"\"\n    form = await request.form()\n    if \"title\" not in form:\n        return None\n    return {name: str(form.get(name) or \"\") for name in EDITOR_FIELDS}\n\n\ndef clean(conn: sqlite3.Connection, page: sqlite3.Row, values: dict) -> dict:\n    \"\"\"Validate the editor's values (everything but the body) without saving.\"\"\"\n    return store.clean_update(conn, page, **{k: v for k, v in values.items()\n                                             if k != \"body_md\"})\n\n\ndef changes(page: sqlite3.Row, cleaned: dict, body_md: str) -> list[str]:\n    \"\"\"Plain-language list of what saving would change.\"\"\"\n    labels = {\"title\": \"Title\", \"slug\": \"Slug\", \"nav_order\": \"Navigation order\"}\n    lines = [f\"{label}: “{page[key]}” → “{cleaned[key]}”\"\n             for key, label in labels.items() if page[key] != cleaned[key]]\n    if page[\"body_md\"] != body_md:\n        lines.append(\"Body: changed (see the preview below)\")\n    return lines or [\"No changes.\"]\n\n\ndef article_context(page: dict) -> dict:\n    \"\"\"What public/_article.html needs to show a Page as Members will see it.\"\"\"\n    return {\"post\": page, \"date_label\": None, \"kind_label\": \"\", \"event_label\": \"\",\n            \"body_html\": render_markdown(page[\"body_md\"])}\n\n\ndef confirmation(request: Request, user: sqlite3.Row, page: sqlite3.Row, *, action: str,\n                 heading: str, summary: list[str], pending: dict | None, button: str,\n                 preview: dict | None = None):\n    \"\"\"The Confirmation screen: nothing has been changed yet (see routes/posts.py).\"\"\"\n    return templates.TemplateResponse(\n        request, \"admin/confirm.html\",\n        {\"title\": heading, \"user\": user, \"summary\": summary, \"pending\": pending or {},\n         \"button\": button,\n         \"action_url\": request.app.url_path_for(action, page_id=page[\"id\"]),\n         \"cancel_url\": request.app.url_path_for(\"back_to_page_editor\", page_id=page[\"id\"]),\n         \"preview\": preview and article_context(preview)})\n\n\ndef editor_page(request, user, page: sqlite3.Row, values: dict | None, error: str):\n    return form_page(request, user, {**dict(page), **(values or {})}, error=error,\n                     status_code=422)\n\n\n@router.get(\"\")\ndef page_list(request: Request, user: sqlite3.Row = Depends(auth.current_user),\n              conn: sqlite3.Connection = Depends(auth.get_db)):\n    return templates.TemplateResponse(\n        request, \"admin/page_list.html\",\n        {\"title\": \"Pages\", \"user\": user, \"pages\": store.list_pages(conn)})\n\n\n@router.get(\"/new\")\ndef new_page(request: Request, user: sqlite3.Row = Depends(auth.current_user)):\n    return form_page(request, user, dict(BLANK))\n\n\n@router.post(\"\")\ndef create_page(request: Request, title: str = Form(\"\"), slug: str = Form(\"\"),\n                body_md: str = Form(\"\"), nav_order: str = Form(\"0\"),\n                user: sqlite3.Row = Depends(auth.current_user),\n                conn: sqlite3.Connection = Depends(auth.get_db)):\n    values = {\"title\": title, \"slug\": slug, \"body_md\": body_md, \"nav_order\": nav_order}\n    try:\n        page_id = store.create_page(conn, author_id=user[\"id\"], **values)\n    except store.PostError as exc:\n        return form_page(request, user, values, error=str(exc), status_code=422)\n    return RedirectResponse(f\"/admin/pages/{page_id}\", status_code=303)\n\n\n@router.get(\"/{page_id}\")\ndef edit_page(request: Request, page_id: int,\n              user: sqlite3.Row = Depends(auth.current_user),\n              conn: sqlite3.Connection = Depends(auth.get_db)):\n    return form_page(request, user, dict(load(conn, page_id)))\n\n\n@router.post(\"/{page_id}\")\ndef save_page(request: Request, page_id: int, confirm: str = Form(\"\"),\n              values: dict | None = Depends(pending_values),\n              user: sqlite3.Row = Depends(auth.current_user),\n              conn: sqlite3.Connection = Depends(auth.get_db)):\n    page = load(conn, page_id)\n    values = values or dict(BLANK)\n    try:\n        cleaned = clean(conn, page, values)\n        if page[\"status\"] == \"published\" and not confirm:\n            return confirmation(\n                request, user, page, action=\"save_page\", button=\"Save changes\",\n                heading=\"Confirm changes to Published page\", pending=values,\n                summary=[\"This page is Published. Saving changes what Members see \"\n                         \"at the next Export.\", *changes(page, cleaned, values[\"body_md\"])],\n                preview={**dict(page), **cleaned, \"body_md\": values[\"body_md\"]})\n        store.update_page(conn, page, **values)\n    except store.PostError as exc:\n        return editor_page(request, user, page, values, str(exc))\n    return RedirectResponse(f\"/admin/pages/{page_id}\", status_code=303)\n\n\n@router.post(\"/{page_id}/edit\")\ndef back_to_page_editor(request: Request, page_id: int,\n                        values: dict | None = Depends(pending_values),\n                        user: sqlite3.Row = Depends(auth.current_user),\n                        conn: sqlite3.Connection = Depends(auth.get_db)):\n    \"\"\"Cancel on a Confirmation screen: the editor again, with the unsaved values.\"\"\"\n    return form_page(request, user, {**dict(load(conn, page_id)), **(values or {})})\n\n\n@router.post(\"/{page_id}/publish\")\ndef publish_page(request: Request, page_id: int, confirm: str = Form(\"\"),\n                 values: dict | None = Depends(pending_values),\n                 user: sqlite3.Row = Depends(auth.current_user),\n                 conn: sqlite3.Connection = Depends(auth.get_db)):\n    page = load(conn, page_id)\n    try:\n        cleaned = clean(conn, page, values) if values is not None else {}\n        if not confirm:\n            shown = {**dict(page), **cleaned,\n                     **({\"body_md\": values[\"body_md\"]} if values is not None else {})}\n            return confirmation(\n                request, user, page, action=\"publish_page\", button=\"Publish\",\n                heading=\"Confirm publishing\", pending=values, preview=shown,\n                summary=[f\"Publish “{shown['title']}”? It stays a Draft until you confirm, \"\n                         \"and reaches the public site at the next Export.\"])\n        if values is not None:  # what's on the editor is what gets published\n            store.update_page(conn, page, **values)\n            page = load(conn, page_id)\n    except store.PostError as exc:\n        return editor_page(request, user, page, values, str(exc))\n    store.publish_page(conn, page)\n    return RedirectResponse(f\"/admin/pages/{page_id}\", status_code=303)\n\n\n@router.post(\"/{page_id}/unpublish\")\ndef unpublish_page(request: Request, page_id: int, confirm: str = Form(\"\"),\n                   values: dict | None = Depends(pending_values),\n                   user: sqlite3.Row = Depends(auth.current_user),\n                   conn: sqlite3.Connection = Depends(auth.get_db)):\n    page = load(conn, page_id)\n    if not confirm:\n        return confirmation(\n            request, user, page, action=\"unpublish_page\", button=\"Unpublish\",\n            heading=\"Confirm unpublishing\", pending=values,\n            summary=[f\"Unpublish “{page['title']}”? It returns to Draft and disappears \"\n                     \"from the public site and its Navigation at the next Export. \"\n                     \"Unsaved edits in the editor are not saved.\"])\n    store.unpublish_page(conn, page)\n    return RedirectResponse(f\"/admin/pages/{page_id}\", status_code=303)\n\n\n@router.post(\"/{page_id}/delete\")\ndef delete_page(request: Request, page_id: int, confirm: str = Form(\"\"),\n                values: dict | None = Depends(pending_values),\n                user: sqlite3.Row = Depends(auth.current_user),\n                conn: sqlite3.Connection = Depends(auth.get_db)):\n    page = load(conn, page_id)\n    if not confirm:\n        return confirmation(\n            request, user, page, action=\"delete_page\", button=\"Delete permanently\",\n            heading=\"Confirm deleting\", pending=values,\n            summary=[f\"Delete “{page['title']}”? Deletion is permanent: the page \"\n                     \"can’t be recovered.\"]\n            + ([\"It leaves the public site at the next Export.\"]\n               if page[\"status\"] == \"published\" else []))\n    store.delete_page(conn, page)\n    return RedirectResponse(request.app.url_path_for(\"page_list\"), status_code=303)\n\n\n@router.get(\"/{page_id}/preview\")\ndef preview_page(request: Request, page_id: int,\n                 conn: sqlite3.Connection = Depends(auth.get_db)):\n    page = load(conn, page_id)\n    return templates.TemplateResponse(\n        request, \"public/post.html\",\n        {\"title\": page[\"title\"], \"lang\": \"es\", \"css_path\": \"/style.css\",\n         \"home_path\": \"/\", **article_context(dict(page))})\n\"\"\"Shared Jinja environment for the console.\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport jinja2\nfrom fastapi.templating import Jinja2Templates\n\nfrom app import settings\nfrom app.auth import csrf_token\nfrom app.timeutil import lima\n\ntemplates = Jinja2Templates(directory=str(settings.TEMPLATES))\ntemplates.env.globals[\"csrf_token\"] = csrf_token\ntemplates.env.filters[\"lima\"] = lima\n\n\n@jinja2.pass_context\ndef path_for(context, name: str, **params) -> str:\n    \"\"\"The path of a named console route, e.g. path_for('edit_post', post_id=3).\n\n    Unlike Starlette's url_for it stays a path (\"/admin/posts/3\"), not a full URL.\n    \"\"\"\n    return context[\"request\"].app.url_path_for(name, **params)\n\n\ntemplates.env.globals[\"path_for\"] = path_for\n\"\"\"Shared test fixtures.\n\nEach test gets its own temporary database, seeded with the demo Admin and\nEditor, so tests never share state.\n\n`client` gives you an anonymous client. `client_as(role)` gives you a client\nthat is signed in as a seeded user of that role, so access-control tests stay\none line:\n\n    def test_editor_cannot_manage_users(client_as):\n        assert client_as(\"editor\").get(\"/admin/users\").status_code == 403\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport os\nimport re\n\nimport pytest\nfrom fastapi.testclient import TestClient\n\n# A developer's real .env must not change test behaviour.\nos.environ.setdefault(\"CMS_ENV_FILE\", os.devnull)\n\nfrom app import settings  # noqa: E402\nfrom app.accounts import seed_users  # noqa: E402\nfrom app.main import create_app  # noqa: E402\n\n# Matches scripts/seed_demo.py. Passwords come from the environment there; in\n# tests they are fixed and meaningless.\nDEMO_USERS = {\n    \"admin\": {\"email\": \"admin@example.test\", \"password\": \"test-admin-pw\"},\n    \"editor\": {\"email\": \"editor@example.test\", \"password\": \"test-editor-pw\"},\n}\n\nCSRF_FIELD = re.compile(r'name=\"csrf_token\" value=\"([^\"]+)\"')\n\n\ndef csrf_from(response) -> str:\n    \"\"\"Pull the CSRF token out of a rendered form.\"\"\"\n    match = CSRF_FIELD.search(response.text)\n    assert match, \"no csrf_token field in the page\"\n    return match.group(1)\n\n\n@pytest.fixture\ndef db_path(tmp_path):\n    path = tmp_path / \"test.db\"\n    seed_users(path, admin_password=DEMO_USERS[\"admin\"][\"password\"],\n authentication, and Deactivation.\n\nUsers are never deleted, only Deactivated (see CONTEXT.md).\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport sqlite3\nfrom datetime import datetime, timezone\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nfrom argon2 import PasswordHasher\nfrom argon2.exceptions import InvalidHashError, VerificationError\n\nfrom app import db\n\n_hasher = PasswordHasher()\n# Verified against when the email is unknown, so a miss costs as much as a hit.\n_DUMMY_HASH = _hasher.hash(\"not-a-real-password\")\n\n\ndef create_user(conn: sqlite3.Connection, *, name: str, email: str,\n                password: str, role: str) -> None:\n    conn.execute(\n        \"INSERT INTO users (name, email, password_hash, role, created_at) \"\n        \"VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?)\",\n        (name, email.strip(), _hasher.hash(password), role,\n         datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat()),\n    )\n    conn.commit()\n\n\ndef authenticate(conn: sqlite3.Connection, email: str,\n                 password: str) -> sqlite3.Row | None:\n    \"\"\"The active User with this email and password, else None (never says why).\"\"\"\n    user = conn.execute(\"SELECT * FROM users WHERE email = ?\",\n                        (email.strip(),)).fetchone()\n    stored = user[\"password_hash\"] if user else _DUMMY_HASH\n    try:\n        _hasher.verify(stored, password)\n    except (VerificationError, InvalidHashError):\n        return None\n    if user is None or not user[\"active\"]:\n        return None\n    return user\n\n\ndef get_user(conn: sqlite3.Connection, user_id: int) -> sqlite3.Row | None:\n    return conn.execute(\"SELECT * FROM users WHERE id = ?\", (user_id,)).fetchone()\n\n\ndef end_sessions(conn: sqlite3.Connection, user_id: int) -> None:\n    \"\"\"Invalidate every session cookie issued to this User.\"\"\"\n    conn.execute(\"UPDATE users SET session_version = session_version + 1 \"\n                 \"WHERE id = ?\", (user_id,))\n    conn.commit()\n\n\ndef set_active(path: Path | str, email: str, active: bool) -> None:\n    \"\"\"Deactivate or reactivate a User. Their content and authorship stay put.\"\"\"\n    conn = db.connect(path)\n    try:\n        conn.execute(\"UPDATE users SET active = ? WHERE email = ?\",\n                     (int(active), email.strip()))\n        conn.commit()\n    finally:\n        conn.close()\n\n\ndef seed_users(path: Path | str, *, admin_password: str, editor_password: str,\n               admin_email: str = \"admin@example.test\",\n               editor_email: str = \"editor@example.test\") -> None:\n    \"\"\"Create the first Admin and Editor. Existing emails are left alone.\"\"\"\n    db.init_db(path)\n    conn = db.connect(path)\n    try:\n        for name, email, password, role in (\n            (\"Demo Admin\", admin_email, admin_password, \"admin\"),\n            (\"Demo Editor\", editor_email, editor_password, \"editor\"),\n        ):\n            exists = conn.execute(\"SELECT 1 FROM users WHERE email = ?\",\n                                  (email,)).fetchone()\n            if not exists:\n                create_user(conn, name=name, email=email, password=password,\n                            role=role)\n    finally:\n        conn.close()\n\"\"\"Permissions and CSRF: the checks shared by every route.\n\n- `csrf_protect` runs on every state-changing request in the app.\n- `current_user` is what makes a console route require login, and refuses a\n  Deactivated User on every request.\n- `require_admin` is the role check for Admin-only areas. Refusal is a 403.\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport hmac\nimport secrets\nimport sqlite3\nfrom typing import Iterator\n\nfrom fastapi import Depends, HTTPException, Request\n\nfrom app import accounts, db\n\nSAFE_METHODS = {\"GET\", \"HEAD\", \"OPTIONS\"}\n\n\nclass LoginRequired(Exception):\n    \"\"\"Raised when a request needs a signed-in User and has none.\"\"\"\n\n\ndef get_db(request: Request) -> Iterator[sqlite3.Connection]:\n    conn = db.connect(request.app.state.db_path)\n    try:\n        yield conn\n    finally:\n        conn.close()\n\n\ndef csrf_token(request: Request) -> str:\n    \"\"\"This session's CSRF token, created on first use.\"\"\"\n    token = request.session.get(\"csrf\")\n    if not token:\n        token = request.session[\"csrf\"] = secrets.token_urlsafe(32)\n    return token\n\n\nasync def csrf_protect(request: Request) -> None:\n    if request.method in SAFE_METHODS:\n        return\n    sent = request.headers.get(\"x-csrf-token\")\n    if sent is None:\n        sent = (await request.form()).get(\"csrf_token\")\n    expected = request.session.get(\"csrf\")\n    if (not expected or not isinstance(sent, str)\n            or not hmac.compare_digest(sent, expected)):\n        raise HTTPException(403, \"Invalid or missing CSRF token.\")\n\n\ndef sign_in(request: Request, user: sqlite3.Row) -> None:\n    # Fresh session and token on login, so nothing set before it carries over.\n    request.session.clear()\n    request.session[\"user_id\"] = user[\"id\"]\n    request.session[\"session_version\"] = user[\"session_version\"]\n    request.session[\"csrf\"] = secrets.token_urlsafe(32)\n\n\ndef current_user(request: Request,\n                 conn: sqlite3.Connection = Depends(get_db)) -> sqlite3.Row:\n    user_id = request.session.get(\"user_id\")\n    user = accounts.get_user(conn, user_id) if user_id else None\n    if (user is None or not user[\"active\"]\n            or user[\"session_version\"] != request.session.get(\"session_version\")):\n        request.session.clear()\n        raise LoginRequired()\n    return user\n\n\ndef require_admin(user: sqlite3.Row = Depends(current_user)) -> sqlite3.Row:\n    if user[\"role\"] != \"admin\":\n        raise HTTPException(403, \"This area is for Admins only.\")\n    return user\n\"\"\"Page list, editor and Preview. Admin only: the router-level check covers every route.\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport sqlite3\n\nfrom fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, Form, HTTPException, Request\nfrom APIRouter(prefix=\"/pages\", dependencies=[Depends(auth.require_admin)])\n\nEDITOR_FIELDS = (\"title\", \"slug\", \"body_md\", \"nav_order\")\nBLANK = {\"title\": \"\", \"slug\": \"\", \"body_md\": \"\", \"nav_order\": \"0\"}\n\n\ndef form_page(request: Request, user: sqlite3.Row, page: dict, *,\n              error: str | None = None, status_code: int = 200):\n    return error},\n        status_code=status_code)\n\n\ndef load(conn: sqlite3.Connection, page_id: int) -> sqlite3.Row:\n    page = store.get_page(conn, page_id)\n    if page is None:\n        raise HTTPException(404, \"Page not found\")\n    return page\n\n\nasync def pending_values(request: Request) -> dict | None:\n    \"\"\"The editor's values as submitted, or None if the form carried for k, v in values.items()\n                                             if k != \"body_md\"})\n\n\ndef changes(page: sqlite3.Row, cleaned: dict, body_md: str) -> list[str]:\n    \"\"\"Plain-language list of what saving would change.\"\"\"\n    labels = {\"title\": \"Title\", \"slug\": \"Slug\", \"nav_order\": \"Navigation order\"}\n    lines = [f\"{label}: “{page[key]}” → “{cleaned[key]}”\"\n It stays a Draft until you confirm, \"\n                         \"and reaches the public site at the next Export.\"])\n        if values is not None:  # what's on the editor is what gets published\n            store.update_page(conn, page, **values)\n            page = load(conn, page_id)\n    except store.PostError as exc:\n        return editor_page(request, user, page, values, str(exc))\n    store.publish_page(conn, page)\n    return RedirectResponse(f\"/admin/pages/{page_id}\", status_code=303)\n\n\n@router.post(\"/{page_id}/unpublish\")\ndef unpublish_page(request: Request, page_id: int, confirm: str = Form(\"\"),\n                   values: dict | None = Depends(pending_values),\n                   user: sqlite3.Row = Depends(auth.current_user),\n                   conn: sqlite3.Connection = Depends(auth.get_db)):\n    page = load(conn, page_id)\n    if not confirm:\n        return confirmation(\n            request, user, page, action=\"unpublish_page\", button=\"Unpublish\",\n            heading=\"Confirm unpublishing\", pending=values,\n            summary=[f\"Unpublish “{page['title']}”? {"parentUuid":"3cb7066c-5c27-445c-91e5-0f3aa94d9b1e","isSidechain":false,"promptId":"55c56ec9-3990-4c96-bb16-48d2cb614d9d","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01MjsZdwZv7K6MbkLGQp5s7J","type":"tool_result","content":"\"\"\"The console: every route here requires a signed-in User.\n\nThe router-level dependency is what guarantees that, so a new route can't\nforget it. Users is an Admin-only placeholder until T07.\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport sqlite3\n\nfrom fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, HTTPException, Request\n\nfrom app import auth, content\nfrom app.routes import pages, posts\nfrom app.web import templates\n\nrouter = APIRouter(prefix=\"/admin\", dependencies=[Depends(auth.current_user)])\n# Before the catch-all 404 at the bottom of this file.\nrouter.include_router(posts.router)\nrouter.include_router(pages.router)\n\n\ndef page(request: Request, template: str, user: sqlite3.Row, title: str, **ctx):\n    return templates.TemplateResponse(request, template,\n                                      {\"title\": title, \"user\": user, **ctx})\n\n\n@router.get(\"\")\ndef dashboard(request: Request, user: sqlite3.Row = Depends(auth.current_user),\n              conn: sqlite3.Connection = Depends(auth.get_db)):\n    return page(request, \"admin/dashboard.html\", user, \"Dashboard\",\n                not_live=content.changes_not_live(conn))\n\n\n@router.get(\"/users\", dependencies=[Depends(auth.require_admin)])\ndef users(request: Request, user: sqlite3.Row = Depends(auth.current_user)):\n    return page(request, \"admin/placeholder.html\", user, \"Users\")\n\n\n@router.get(\"/{path:path}\")\ndef not_found(path: str):\n    # Signed-out visitors were already redirected; this is a real 404.\n    raise HTTPException(404, \"Not found\")\n\"\"\"The FastAPI application.\n\nThe console lives under /admin (plus /login and /logout); the public site\nanswers at /. Add routes in their own modules under app/routes/ and include\nthem here. Keep this file small.\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nfrom fastapi import Depends, FastAPI, Request\nfrom fastapi.responses import RedirectResponse, Response\nfrom starlette.middleware.sessions import SessionMiddleware\n\nfrom app import auth, db, publish, settings\nfrom app.routes import auth as auth_routes\nfrom app.routes import console\nfrom app.web import templates\n\n\ndef create_app(database_path: Path | str | None = None) -> FastAPI:\n    db_path = Path(database_path) if database_path else settings.DATABASE_PATH\n    db.init_db(db_path)\n\n    app = FastAPI(title=\"IPHS 400 MP2 CMS\",\n                  dependencies=[Depends(auth.csrf_protect)])\n    app.state.db_path = db_path\n    app.add_middleware(SessionMiddleware, secret_key=settings.SECRET_KEY,\n                       session_cookie=\"cms_session\", same_site=\"lax\",\n                       max_age=8 * 60 * 60)\n\n    @app.exception_handler(auth.LoginRequired)\n    def to_login(request: Request, exc: auth.LoginRequired):\n        return RedirectResponse(\"/login\", status_code=303)\n\n    @app.get(\"/style.css\")\n    def stylesheet():\n        # Lets the console's Preview load the public stylesheet.\n        return Response(publish.CSS, media_type=\"text/css\")\n\n    @app.get(\"/\")\n    def public_home(request: Request):\n        return templates.TemplateResponse(\n            request, \"public/home.html\",\n            {\"title\": settings.SITE_TITLE, \"news\": []},\n        )\n\n    app.include_router(auth_routes.router)\n    app.include_router(console.router)\n    return app\n\"\"\"SQLite access. No ORM (ADR-002): a connection per request, plain SQL.\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport sqlite3\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nSCHEMA = \"\"\"\nCREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS users (\n    id              INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,\n    name            TEXT    NOT NULL,\n    email           TEXT    NOT NULL UNIQUE COLLATE NOCASE,\n    password_hash   TEXT    NOT NULL,\n    role            TEXT    NOT NULL CHECK (role IN ('admin', 'editor')),\n    active          INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,\n    session_version INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,\n    created_at      TEXT    NOT NULL\n);\n\nCREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS posts (\n    id                INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,\n    kind              TEXT    NOT NULL CHECK (kind IN ('news', 'event', 'menu')),\n    title             TEXT    NOT NULL,\n    slug              TEXT    NOT NULL,\n    body_md           TEXT    NOT NULL DEFAULT '',\n    status            TEXT    NOT NULL DEFAULT 'draft'\n                              CHECK (status IN ('draft', 'published')),\n    author_id         INTEGER NOT NULL REFERENCES users (id),\n    created_at        TEXT    NOT NULL,\n    updated_at        TEXT    NOT NULL,\n    first_published_at TEXT,\n    event_start_date  TEXT,\n    event_start_time  TEXT,\n    event_end_date    TEXT,\n    UNIQUE (kind, slug)\n);\n\nCREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS pages (\n    id                 INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,\n    title              TEXT    NOT NULL,\n    slug               TEXT    NOT NULL UNIQUE,\n    body_md            TEXT    NOT NULL DEFAULT '',\n    status             TEXT    NOT NULL DEFAULT 'draft'\n                               CHECK (status IN ('draft', 'published')),\n    nav_order          INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,\n    author_id          INTEGER NOT NULL REFERENCES users (id),\n    created_at         TEXT    NOT NULL,\n    updated_at         TEXT    NOT NULL,\n    first_published_at TEXT\n);\n\nCREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS exports (\n    id     INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,\n    ran_at TEXT NOT NULL\n);\n\n-- One row per Post or Page that had been Published and was then deleted. Counted as a\n-- change not yet Live until the next Export.\nCREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS deleted_published (\n    id         INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,\n    deleted_at TEXT NOT NULL\n);\n\"\"\"\n\n\ndef connect(path: Path | str) -> sqlite3.Connection:\n    # Sync FastAPI dependencies and endpoints may run on different threads.\n    conn = sqlite3.connect(str(path), check_same_thread=False)\n    conn.row_factory = sqlite3.Row\n    conn.execute(\"PRAGMA foreign_keys = ON\")\n    return conn\n\n\nEVENT_COLUMNS = (\"event_start_date\", \"event_start_time\", \"event_end_date\")\n\n\ndef init_db(path: Path | str) -> None:\n    conn = connect(path)\n    try:\n        conn.executescript(SCHEMA)\n        # A database made before T04 has no Event date columns.\n        have = {row[\"name\"] for row in conn.execute(\"PRAGMA table_info(posts)\")}\n        for column in EVENT_COLUMNS:\n            if column not in have:\n                conn.execute(f\"ALTER TABLE posts ADD COLUMN {column} TEXT\")\n        conn.commit()\n    finally:\n        conn.close()\n\"\"\"Pages: standing content with a navigation entry. Shared by the console and Export.\n\nSame Draft/Published lifecycle and Slug rules as Posts (see content.py), but a\nPage Slug is unique across all Pages and can't be one of the public site's own\naddresses.\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport sqlite3\n\nfrom app.content import PostError, _clean_title, slugify\nfrom app.timeutil import utc_now\n\nRESERVED_SLUGS = (\"index\", \"noticias\", \"eventos\", \"menus\", \"style\")\n\n\ndef _taken(conn: sqlite3.Connection, slug: str, except_id: int | None) -> bool:\n    return conn.execute(\"SELECT 1 FROM pages WHERE slug = ? AND id IS NOT ?\",\n                        (slug, except_id)).fetchone() is not None\n\n\ndef _reserved(slug: str) -> PostError:\n    return PostError(f\"The slug “{slug}” is reserved for the public site. Choose another.\")\n\n\ndef _slug_for(conn: sqlite3.Connection, title: str, typed: str,\n              except_id: int | None = None) -> str:\n    \"\"\"A typed Slug must be free; a generated one gets -2, -3, ... until it is.\"\"\"\n    if typed.strip():\n        slug = slugify(typed)\n        if not slug:\n            raise PostError(\"The slug needs letters or numbers.\")\n        if slug in RESERVED_SLUGS:\n            raise _reserved(slug)\n        if _taken(conn, slug, except_id):\n            raise PostError(f\"The slug “{slug}” is already used by another page.\")\n        return slug\n    base = slugify(title)\n    if not base:\n        raise PostError(\"The title needs letters or numbers to make a slug from.\")\n    if base in RESERVED_SLUGS:\n        raise _reserved(base)\n    slug, n = base, 1\n    while _taken(conn, slug, except_id):\n        n += 1\n        slug = f\"{base}-{n}\"\n    return slug\n\n\ndef _nav_order(value: str) -> int:\n\"\"\"Login and logout.\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport sqlite3\n\nfrom fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, Form, Request\nfrom fastapi.responses import RedirectResponse\n\nfrom app import
